    @Test
    void installersOrderFinalHandoffAfterManagedWorkerSetupInSource() throws Exception {
        // given
        String posixInstaller = Files.readString(Path.of("install.sh"));
        String powershellInstaller = Files.readString(Path.of("install.ps1"));
        String posixOnboarding =
                posixInstaller.substring(posixInstaller.lastIndexOf("if [[ \"$SETUP_PHASE_PLANNED\" == true ]]"));
        String powershellOnboarding =
                powershellInstaller.substring(powershellInstaller.lastIndexOf("if ($SetupPhasePlanned)"));
        String posixAutostartFilter = posixInstaller.substring(
                posixInstaller.indexOf("autostart_environment_name()"),
                posixInstaller.indexOf("has_control_character()"));
        String powershellAutostartFilter = powershellInstaller.substring(
                powershellInstaller.indexOf("function Test-AutostartEnvironmentName"),
                powershellInstaller.indexOf("function Write-AutostartEnvironmentFile"));
        String powershellManagedWorkerBoundary = powershellInstaller.substring(
                powershellInstaller.indexOf("function Start-ManagedWorkersWithoutInstallerCompletion"),
                powershellInstaller.indexOf("function Get-InstalledAppVersionFallback"));

        // when
        int posixDeferredSetup = posixOnboarding.indexOf("run_setup_local_with_deferred_completion");
        int posixManagedWorkers =
                posixOnboarding.indexOf("start_managed_workers_without_installer_completion", posixDeferredSetup);
        int posixFinalHandoff = posixOnboarding.indexOf("print_installer_completion");
        int powershellDeferredSetup = powershellOnboarding.indexOf("Set-InstallerCompletionMode \"defer\"");
        int powershellManagedWorkers =
                powershellOnboarding.indexOf("Start-ManagedWorkersWithoutInstallerCompletion", powershellDeferredSetup);
        int powershellFinalHandoff = powershellOnboarding.indexOf("Set-InstallerCompletionMode \"print\"");

        // then
        assertThat(posixDeferredSetup).isNotNegative().isLessThan(posixManagedWorkers);
        assertThat(posixManagedWorkers).isLessThan(posixFinalHandoff);
        assertThat(powershellDeferredSetup).isNotNegative().isLessThan(powershellManagedWorkers);
        assertThat(powershellManagedWorkers).isLessThan(powershellFinalHandoff);
        assertThat(powershellOnboarding.indexOf("Clear-InstallerCompletionMode"))
                .isBetween(powershellDeferredSetup, powershellManagedWorkers);
        assertThat(powershellOnboarding.indexOf("Restore-InstallerCompletionEnvironment"))
                .isGreaterThan(powershellFinalHandoff);
        assertThat(powershellOnboarding).doesNotContain("if ($DryRun)");
        assertThat(posixInstaller)
                .contains(
                        "local -x \"$INSTALLER_COMPLETION_ENV=defer\"",
                        "local -x \"$INSTALLER_COMPLETION_ENV=print\"",
                        "unset \"$INSTALLER_COMPLETION_ENV\"")
                .doesNotContain(
                        "local -x SYMPHONY_TRELLO_INSTALLER_COMPLETION=", "unset SYMPHONY_TRELLO_INSTALLER_COMPLETION");
        assertThat(posixAutostartFilter).contains("[[ \"$name\" != \"$INSTALLER_COMPLETION_ENV\" ]] || return 1");
        assertThat(powershellAutostartFilter)
                .contains("if ($Name -eq $InstallerCompletionEnvironmentName)", "return $false");
        assertThat(powershellManagedWorkerBoundary)
                .containsSubsequence(
                        "try {",
                        "Clear-InstallerCompletionMode",
                        "Start-ManagedWorkers",
                        "} finally {",
                        "Restore-InstallerCompletionEnvironment");
        assertThat(powershellOnboarding.substring(powershellOnboarding.indexOf("elseif ($WorkerPhasePlanned)")))
                .contains("Start-ManagedWorkersWithoutInstallerCompletion");
    }

    @Test
    void installersOfferPathSetupBeforeGuidedSetupCanAbort() throws Exception {
        // given
        String posixInstaller = Files.readString(Path.of("install.sh"));
        String powershellInstaller = Files.readString(Path.of("install.ps1"));

        // when
        int posixPathSetup = posixInstaller.indexOf("\noffer_path_setup\n\nif [[ \"$SETUP_PHASE_PLANNED\" == true ]]");
        int powershellPathSetup = powershellInstaller.indexOf("\nOffer-PathSetup\n\nif ($SetupPhasePlanned)");

        // then
        assertThat(posixPathSetup)
                .as("POSIX installer must offer PATH setup before setup-local or restart can abort")
                .isNotNegative();
        assertThat(powershellPathSetup)
                .as("PowerShell installer must offer PATH setup before setup-local or restart can abort")
                .isNotNegative();
    }

    @Test
    void posixInstallerAddsPathSetupAfterInstallingCodexWithUserLocalNpm() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("script"));
        Path installScript = Path.of("install.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path sourceRepository = createSourceRepository(temporaryDirectory);
        Path fakeBin = createFakeToolchain(temporaryDirectory);
        Files.delete(fakeBin.resolve("codex"));
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "bash", "/bin/bash");
        writeExecutable(
                fakeBin.resolve("git"),
                """
                #!/usr/bin/env bash
                exec /usr/bin/git "$@"
                """);
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "mkdir", "/bin/mkdir");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "uname", "/bin/uname");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "chmod", "/bin/chmod");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "dirname", "/usr/bin/dirname");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "sed", "/bin/sed");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "head", "/usr/bin/head");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "cat", "/bin/cat");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "ln", "/bin/ln");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "tr", "/usr/bin/tr");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "sleep", "/bin/sleep");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "nohup", "/usr/bin/nohup");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "env", "/usr/bin/env");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "rm", "/bin/rm");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "basename", "/usr/bin/basename");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "sha256sum", "/usr/bin/sha256sum");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "awk", "/usr/bin/awk");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "grep", "/bin/grep");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "mktemp", "/usr/bin/mktemp");
        writeCommandProxy(fakeBin, "mv", "/bin/mv");
        writeExecutable(
                fakeBin.resolve("npm"),
                """
                #!/usr/bin/env bash
                set -euo pipefail
                prefix=""
                while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
                  case "${1:-}" in
                    --prefix) prefix="${2:?missing prefix}"; shift 2 ;;
                    *) shift ;;
                  esac
                done
                mkdir -p "$prefix/bin"
                printf '#!/usr/bin/env bash\\nexit 0\\n' > "$prefix/bin/codex"
                chmod +x "$prefix/bin/codex"
                echo "npm $*" >> "${SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG:?}"
                """);
        Path symphonyHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("npm-codex-home");
        Path binDirectory = temporaryDirectory.resolve("npm-codex-bin");
        Path fakeLog = temporaryDirectory.resolve("npm-codex-fake-tools.log");
        Path uninstallScript = Path.of("uninstall.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path home = temporaryDirectory.resolve("npm-codex-user-home");
        Map<String, String> environment = Map.of(
                "PATH", fakeBin.toString(),
                "HOME", home.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REPO_URL", sourceRepository.toUri().toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_HOME", symphonyHome.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG", fakeLog.toString(),
                "SHELL", "/bin/bash");

        // when
        PseudoTerminalResult install = runWithPseudoTerminal(
                environment,
                "y\napi-key\napi-token\nNpm Codex Board\n\n\n",
                "bash " + shellQuote(installScript.toString()) + " --bin-dir " + shellQuote(binDirectory.toString()));
        Map<String, String> uninstallEnvironment = new LinkedHashMap<>(environment);
        uninstallEnvironment.put("PATH", fakeBin + ":/bin:/usr/bin");
        ProcessResult uninstall = run(
                uninstallEnvironment,
                "bash",
                uninstallScript.toString(),
                "--yes",
                "--bin-dir",
                binDirectory.toString());

        // then
        assertThat(install.exitCode()).as(install.transcript()).isZero();
        assertThat(uninstall.exitCode()).as(uninstall.output()).isZero();
        assertThat(install.transcript())
                .contains(
                        "Install Codex CLI with Symphony-managed npm.",
                        "Install location: " + symphonyHome.resolve("npm"),
                        "This keeps system-wide npm packages unchanged.",
                        "Added " + binDirectory + " to PATH in " + home.resolve(".bashrc"),
                        "Added " + binDirectory + " to PATH in " + home.resolve(".profile"));
        assertThat(binDirectory.resolve("codex")).doesNotExist();
        assertThat(symphonyHome.resolve("npm")).doesNotExist();
    }

    @Test
    void posixInstallerUsesSystemdUserDirectoryAndXdgConfigAutostartEnvironment() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("script"));
        Path installScript = Path.of("install.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path uninstallScript = Path.of("uninstall.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path sourceRepository = createSourceRepository(temporaryDirectory);
        Path fakeBin = createFakeToolchain(temporaryDirectory);
        Path home = temporaryDirectory.resolve("xdg-systemd-home");
        Path xdgDataHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("xdg-systemd-data");
        Path xdgConfigHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("xdg systemd config");
        Path xdgStateHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("xdg-systemd-state");
        Path xdgCacheHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("xdg-systemd-cache");
        Path binDirectory = temporaryDirectory.resolve("xdg-systemd-bin");
        Path fakeLog = temporaryDirectory.resolve("xdg-systemd.log");
        Path servicePath = xdgConfigHome.resolve("systemd/user/symphony-trello.service");
        Path homeServicePath = home.resolve(".config/systemd/user/symphony-trello.service");
        Path autostartEnvPath = xdgConfigHome.resolve("symphony-trello/autostart.env");
        Files.createDirectories(home);
        Files.createFile(temporaryDirectory.resolve("codex-authenticated"));
        Map<String, String> installEnvironment = Map.of(
                "PATH",
                fakeBin + File.pathSeparator + System.getenv("PATH"),
                "HOME",
                home.toString(),
                "XDG_DATA_HOME",
                xdgDataHome.toString(),
                "XDG_CONFIG_HOME",
                xdgConfigHome.toString(),
                "XDG_STATE_HOME",
                xdgStateHome.toString(),
                "XDG_CACHE_HOME",
                xdgCacheHome.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REPO_URL",
                sourceRepository.toUri().toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REF",
                "main",
                "SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG",
                fakeLog.toString());

        // when
        PseudoTerminalResult install = runWithPseudoTerminal(
                installEnvironment,
                "api-key\napi-token\nXDG Systemd Board\n\n\n",
                "bash " + shellQuote(installScript.toString()) + " --no-update-path --bin-dir "
                        + shellQuote(binDirectory.toString()));
        install.assertSuccess();
        String serviceContent = Files.readString(servicePath);
        String installContext = Files.readString(xdgStateHome.resolve("symphony-trello/install-context.properties"));
        Map<String, String> uninstallEnvironment = Map.of(
                "PATH",
                fakeBin + File.pathSeparator + System.getenv("PATH"),
                "HOME",
                home.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG",
                fakeLog.toString());
        ProcessResult uninstall = run(
                uninstallEnvironment,
                "bash",
                uninstallScript.toString(),
                "--yes",
                "--bin-dir",
                binDirectory.toString());

        // then
        uninstall.assertSuccess();
        assertThat(servicePath).doesNotExist();
        assertThat(homeServicePath).doesNotExist();
        assertThat(autostartEnvPath).doesNotExist();
        assertThat(serviceContent)
                .contains("EnvironmentFile=-" + autostartEnvPath.toString().replace(" ", "\\x20"))
                .doesNotContain("%h/.config/symphony-trello/autostart.env");
        assertThat(installContext)
                .contains(
                        "systemd_user_dir=" + xdgConfigHome.resolve("systemd/user"),
                        "systemd_service_path=" + servicePath,
                        "autostart_env_path=" + autostartEnvPath);
        assertThat(fakeLog)
                .content(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                .contains(
                        "systemctl --user enable symphony-trello.service",
                        "systemctl --user restart symphony-trello.service",
                        "systemctl --user disable --now symphony-trello.service");
    }

    @MethodSource("posixInstallerHandoffScenarios")
    @ParameterizedTest(name = "{0}")
    void posixInstallerPrintsHandoffOnlyAfterManagedWorkerOutcome(InstallerHandoffScenario scenario) throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("script"));
        Path installScript = Path.of("install.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path sourceRepository = createSourceRepository(temporaryDirectory);
        Path fakeBin = createFakeToolchain(temporaryDirectory);
        Path home = temporaryDirectory.resolve("handoff-" + scenario.slug() + "-home");
        Path symphonyHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("handoff-" + scenario.slug() + "-symphony-home");
        Path binDirectory = temporaryDirectory.resolve("handoff-" + scenario.slug() + "-bin");
        Path fakeLog = temporaryDirectory.resolve("handoff-" + scenario.slug() + ".log");
        Files.createDirectories(home);
        Files.createFile(temporaryDirectory.resolve("codex-authenticated"));
        Map<String, String> environment = new LinkedHashMap<>(Map.of(
                "PATH",
                fakeBin + File.pathSeparator + System.getenv("PATH"),
                "HOME",
                home.toString(),
                "USER",
                "symphony-test",
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REPO_URL",
                sourceRepository.toUri().toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REF",
                "main",
                "SYMPHONY_HOME",
                symphonyHome.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG",
                fakeLog.toString()));
        environment.putAll(scenario.environment());

        try {

            // when
            PseudoTerminalResult result = runWithPseudoTerminal(
                    environment,
                    "api-key\napi-token\nHandoff Queue\n",
                    "bash " + shellQuote(installScript.toString()) + " --no-update-path --bin-dir "
                            + shellQuote(binDirectory.toString()));

            // then
            assertThat(result.transcript())
                    .containsSubsequence(scenario.expectedOutput().toArray(String[]::new));
            if (scenario.success()) {
                result.assertSuccess();
                assertThat(result.transcript())
                        .containsOnlyOnce("You're good to go - your Trello board is now a queue for Codex work.");
                assertThat(result.transcript())
                        .containsSubsequence(
                                "You're good to go - your Trello board is now a queue for Codex work.",
                                "Connected board: Handoff Queue",
                                "Workflow: " + symphonyHome.resolve("config/WORKFLOW.handoff-queue.md"),
                                "symphony-trello logs --workflow '"
                                        + symphonyHome.resolve("config/WORKFLOW.handoff-queue.md") + "'",
                                GuidedTutorial.OFFER_PROMPT,
                                "You can start it later with:",
                                "  symphony-trello tutorial");
            } else {
                assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.transcript()).isNotZero();
                assertThat(result.transcript()).doesNotContain("You're good to go", "Useful commands:");
            }
        } finally {
            Path installedCommand = binDirectory.resolve("symphony-trello");
            if (Files.isExecutable(installedCommand)) {
                run(environment, installedCommand.toString(), "stop").assertSuccess();
            }
        }
    }

    private static Stream<InstallerHandoffScenario> posixInstallerHandoffScenarios() {
        return Stream.of(
                new InstallerHandoffScenario(
                        "lingering failure precedes handoff",
                        Map.of("SYMPHONY_FAKE_LINGER_FAILURE", "1"),
                        true,
                        List.of(
                                "[4/4] Starting managed workers",
                                "User systemd service enabled: symphony-trello.service",
                                "Could not enable user lingering. The service will start when the user session starts.",
                                "You're good to go - your Trello board is now a queue for Codex work.")),
                new InstallerHandoffScenario(
                        "unavailable service manager falls back before handoff",
                        Map.of("SYMPHONY_FAKE_SYSTEMD_UNAVAILABLE", "1"),
                        true,
                        List.of(
                                "[4/4] Starting managed workers",
                                "User systemd is unavailable in this session.",
                                "Autostart service was not configured.",
                                "start --all",
                                "You're good to go - your Trello board is now a queue for Codex work.")),
                new InstallerHandoffScenario(
                        "failed service setup falls back before handoff",
                        Map.of("SYMPHONY_FAKE_SYSTEMD_ENABLE_FAILURE", "1"),
                        true,
                        List.of(
                                "[4/4] Starting managed workers",
                                "Could not enable the user systemd service. Falling back to direct start.",
                                "Autostart service was not configured.",
                                "start --all",
                                "You're good to go - your Trello board is now a queue for Codex work.")),
                new InstallerHandoffScenario(
                        "failed fallback start suppresses handoff",
                        Map.of("SYMPHONY_FAKE_START_ALL_FAILURE", "1"),
                        false,
                        List.of(
                                "[4/4] Starting managed workers",
                                "Could not enable the user systemd service. Falling back to direct start.",
                                "Autostart service was not configured.",
                                "start --all",
                                "managed start --all failed",
                                "Installer stopped during [4/4] Starting managed workers.",
                                "start --all")));
    }

    private record InstallerHandoffScenario(
            String name, Map<String, String> environment, boolean success, List<String> expectedOutput) {
        String slug() {
            return name.replace(' ', '-');
        }

        @Override
        public String toString() {
            return name;
        }
    }

    @ParameterizedTest(name = "pinned ref type: {0}")
    @ValueSource(strings = {"tag", "sha"})
    void posixInstallerRerunsPinnedNonBranchRefsWithoutPulling(String refType) throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        Path installScript = Path.of("install.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path sourceRepository = createSourceRepository(temporaryDirectory);
        String ref = pinnedRef(sourceRepository, refType);
        Path fakeBin = createFakeToolchain(temporaryDirectory);
        Path symphonyHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("pinned-" + refType + "-home");
        Path installPrefix = symphonyHome.resolve("app");
        Path binDirectory = temporaryDirectory.resolve("pinned-" + refType + "-bin");
        Path fakeLog = temporaryDirectory.resolve("pinned-" + refType + "-fake-tools.log");
        Map<String, String> environment = Map.of(
                "PATH", fakeBin + System.getProperty("path.separator") + System.getenv("PATH"),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REPO_URL", sourceRepository.toUri().toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REF", ref,
                "SYMPHONY_HOME", symphonyHome.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG", fakeLog.toString());

        // when
        ProcessResult install = run(
                environment, "bash", installScript.toString(), "--no-onboard", "--bin-dir", binDirectory.toString());
        addSourceRepositoryCommit(sourceRepository, "AFTER_PINNED_REF", "newer\n");
        ProcessResult rerun = run(
                environment, "bash", installScript.toString(), "--no-onboard", "--bin-dir", binDirectory.toString());

        // then
        assertThat(install.exitCode()).as(install.output()).isZero();
        assertThat(rerun.exitCode()).as(rerun.output()).isZero();
        assertThat(installPrefix.resolve("AFTER_PINNED_REF")).doesNotExist();
    }

    @Test
    void posixInstallerUpdateRunsCleanBuildAndRestartsManagedWorkers() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("script"));
        Path installScript = Path.of("install.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path sourceRepository = createSourceRepository(temporaryDirectory);
        Path fakeBin = createFakeToolchain(temporaryDirectory);
        Path symphonyHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("update-restart-home");
        Path binDirectory = temporaryDirectory.resolve("update-restart-bin");
        Path fakeLog = temporaryDirectory.resolve("update-restart-fake-tools.log");
        Files.createFile(temporaryDirectory.resolve("codex-authenticated"));
        Map<String, String> environment = Map.of(
                "PATH", fakeBin + System.getProperty("path.separator") + System.getenv("PATH"),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REPO_URL", sourceRepository.toUri().toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_HOME", symphonyHome.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG", fakeLog.toString());

        try {

            // when
            PseudoTerminalResult install = runWithPseudoTerminal(
                    environment,
                    "api-key\napi-token\nDocs Queue\n\n\n",
                    "bash " + shellQuote(installScript.toString()) + " --bin-dir "
                            + shellQuote(binDirectory.toString()));
            addSourceRepositoryCommit(sourceRepository, "UPDATED", "updated\n");
            PseudoTerminalResult update = runWithPseudoTerminal(
                    environment,
                    "api-key\napi-token\nDocs Queue\n\n\n",
                    "bash " + shellQuote(installScript.toString()) + " --bin-dir "
                            + shellQuote(binDirectory.toString()));

            // then
            assertThat(install.exitCode()).as(install.transcript()).isZero();
            assertThat(update.exitCode()).as(update.transcript()).isZero();
            assertThat(install.transcript()).doesNotContain("Checking generated workflows...");
            assertThat(update.transcript())
                    .containsSubsequence(
                            "Update plan",
                            "[2/4] Updating Symphony",
                            "Stopped WORKFLOW.docs-queue.md",
                            "OK  Managed workers stopped for the update",
                            "Checking generated workflows...",
                            "[3/4] Running setup",
                            "[4/4] Restarting managed workers",
                            "You're good to go");
            assertThat(fakeLog)
                    .content(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                    .contains("mvnw -q -f " + symphonyHome.resolve("app/pom.xml") + " -DskipTests clean package")
                    .contains("TrelloBoardSetupMain migrate-workflows --from-version " + FAKE_APP_VERSION + " dotenv=")
                    .doesNotContain("migrate-workflows --from-version " + FAKE_APP_VERSION + " --non-interactive")
                    .containsSubsequence(
                            "TrelloBoardSetupMain stop",
                            "mvnw -q -f " + symphonyHome.resolve("app/pom.xml") + " -DskipTests clean package",
                            "TrelloBoardSetupMain migrate-workflows --from-version " + FAKE_APP_VERSION,
                            "TrelloBoardSetupMain setup-local",
                            "TrelloBoardSetupMain start",
                            "--all");
        } finally {
            if (Files.exists(binDirectory.resolve("symphony-trello"))) {
                run(environment, binDirectory.resolve("symphony-trello").toString(), "stop");
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void posixNoOnboardUpdateRestartsStoppedWorkersWithStartAll() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("script"));
        Path installScript = Path.of("install.sh").toAbsolutePath();
        Path sourceRepository = createSourceRepository(temporaryDirectory);
        Path fakeBin = createFakeToolchain(temporaryDirectory);
        Path symphonyHome = temporaryDirectory.resolve("no-onboard-update-home");
        Path binDirectory = temporaryDirectory.resolve("no-onboard-update-bin");
        Path fakeLog = temporaryDirectory.resolve("no-onboard-update-fake-tools.log");
        Files.createFile(temporaryDirectory.resolve("codex-authenticated"));
        Map<String, String> environment = Map.of(
                "PATH", fakeBin + System.getProperty("path.separator") + System.getenv("PATH"),
                "SYMPHONY_TRELLO_REPO_URL", sourceRepository.toUri().toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_HOME", symphonyHome.toString(),
                "SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG", fakeLog.toString());

        try {
            PseudoTerminalResult install = runWithPseudoTerminal(
                    environment,
                    "n\napi-key\napi-token\nDocs Queue\n",
                    "bash " + shellQuote(installScript.toString()) + " --bin-dir "
                            + shellQuote(binDirectory.toString()));
            addSourceRepositoryCommit(sourceRepository, "UPDATED", "updated\n");

            // when
            ProcessResult update = run(
                    environment,
                    "bash",
                    installScript.toString(),
                    "--no-onboard",
                    "--bin-dir",
                    binDirectory.toString());
            Map<String, String> withoutMigrationCommand = new LinkedHashMap<>(environment);
            withoutMigrationCommand.put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_WITHOUT_WORKFLOW_MIGRATION", "true");
            withoutMigrationCommand.put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_MIGRATION_HELP", migrationHelp(false));
            ProcessResult updateWithoutMigrationCommand = run(
                    withoutMigrationCommand,
                    "bash",
                    installScript.toString(),
                    "--no-onboard",
                    "--bin-dir",
                    binDirectory.toString());
            Map<String, String> failingMigration = new LinkedHashMap<>(environment);
            failingMigration.put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_WORKFLOW_MIGRATION_FAILURE", "true");
            ProcessResult rejectedMigration = run(
                    failingMigration,
                    "bash",
                    installScript.toString(),
                    "--no-onboard",
                    "--bin-dir",
                    binDirectory.toString());

            // then
            assertThat(install.exitCode()).as(install.transcript()).isZero();
            assertThat(update.exitCode()).as(update.output()).isZero();
            assertThat(update.output())
                    .containsSubsequence(
                            "[2/3] Updating Symphony",
                            "OK  Managed workers stopped for the update",
                            "Checking generated workflows...",
                            "[3/3] Restarting managed workers",
                            "Symphony for Trello updated.",
                            "Next step: check the managed workers with:",
                            "status");
            assertThat(fakeLog)
                    .content(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                    .containsSubsequence(
                            "TrelloBoardSetupMain stop",
                            "TrelloBoardSetupMain migrate-workflows --from-version " + FAKE_APP_VERSION
                                    + " --non-interactive",
                            "TrelloBoardSetupMain start",
                            "--all");
            assertThat(updateWithoutMigrationCommand.exitCode())
                    .as(updateWithoutMigrationCommand.output())
                    .isZero();
            assertThat(updateWithoutMigrationCommand.output())
                    .contains("NOTE  This Symphony version cannot check generated workflow bodies.")
                    .doesNotContain("migrate-workflows --from-version");
            assertThat(rejectedMigration.exitCode()).isNotZero();
            assertThat(rejectedMigration.output())
                    .contains("REJECTED  simulated workflow migration rejection")
                    .doesNotContain("[3/3] Restarting managed workers");
        } finally {
            if (Files.exists(binDirectory.resolve("symphony-trello"))) {
                run(environment, binDirectory.resolve("symphony-trello").toString(), "stop");
            }
        }
    }

    @CsvSource({
        "false, false, fail", "true, false, fail",
        "false, true, fail", "true, true, fail",
        "false, false, malformed", "true, false, malformed",
        "false, true, malformed", "true, true, malformed",
        "false, false, multiline", "true, false, multiline",
        "false, true, multiline", "true, true, multiline"
    })
    @ParameterizedTest
    void installersRejectAnUnprovenInstalledVersion(boolean powershell, boolean fromSource, String mode)
            throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(false);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION_MODE", mode);

        // when
        ProcessResult result = fromSource
                ? source.run(launcher)
                : powershell
                        ? source.installed().runPowerShell(powershellCommand())
                        : source.installed().runPosix();

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output()))
                .contains("cannot determine installed Symphony version", "repair Java")
                .doesNotContain("Checking generated workflows", "Symphony for Trello updated.");
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @CsvSource({
        "false, src/main/java/Local.java, false", "true, src/main/java/Local.java, false",
        "false, src/main/resources/local.properties, true", "true, src/main/resources/local.properties, true",
        "false, .mvn/jvm.config, true", "true, .mvn/jvm.config, true",
        "false, .mvn/maven.config, false", "true, .mvn/maven.config, false",
        "false, .codex/skills/local/SKILL.md, true", "true, .codex/skills/local/SKILL.md, true",
        "false, .codex/skills/TESSL__local/SKILL.md, false", "true, .codex/skills/TESSL__local/SKILL.md, false",
        "false, .env, true", "true, .env, true",
        "false, config/application.properties, false", "true, config/application.properties, false",
        "false, oss-fuzz/corpora/RepositorySourceFuzzer/local, true",
                "true, oss-fuzz/corpora/RepositorySourceFuzzer/local, true"
    })
    @ParameterizedTest
    void installersRejectUntrackedBuildInputsBeforeUpdating(boolean powershell, String relativePath, boolean ignored)
            throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(true);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        Path app = source.installed().appHome();
        Path localInput = app.resolve(relativePath);
        Files.createDirectories(localInput.getParent());
        Files.writeString(localInput, "local build input");
        if (ignored) {
            Files.writeString(app.resolve(".git/info/exclude"), relativePath + "\n");
        }

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output())).contains("source checkout has untracked build inputs");
        assertThat(localInput).hasContent("local build input");
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @CsvSource({
        "false, .codex/skills/extra, SKILL.md",
        "true, .codex/skills/extra, SKILL.md",
        "false, .codex/skills/.hidden, SKILL.md",
        "true, .codex/skills/.hidden, SKILL.md",
        "false, config, application.properties",
        "true, config, application.properties",
        "false, .codex, skills/extra/SKILL.md",
        "true, .codex, skills/extra/SKILL.md",
        "false, .codex/skills, extra/SKILL.md",
        "true, .codex/skills, extra/SKILL.md",
        "false, oss-fuzz, corpora/RepositorySourceFuzzer/local",
        "true, oss-fuzz, corpora/RepositorySourceFuzzer/local"
    })
    @ParameterizedTest
    void installersRejectUntrackedBuildInputLinks(boolean powershell, String relativeLink, String relativeInput)
            throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeFalse(isWindows());
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(true);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        Path external = temporaryDirectory.resolve("external-input");
        Path input = external.resolve(relativeInput);
        Files.createDirectories(input.getParent());
        Files.writeString(input, "external build input");
        Path link = source.installed().appHome().resolve(relativeLink);
        Files.createDirectories(link.getParent());
        Files.createSymbolicLink(link, external);

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output())).contains("source checkout has untracked build inputs");
        assertThat(link).isSymbolicLink();
        assertThat(input).hasContent("external build input");
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @CsvSource({
        "false, .codex/skills/local, SKILL.md", "true, .codex/skills/local, SKILL.md",
        "false, .codex, skills/local/SKILL.md", "true, .codex, skills/local/SKILL.md",
        "false, config, application.properties", "true, config, application.properties",
        "false, oss-fuzz, corpora/RepositorySourceFuzzer/local", "true, oss-fuzz, corpora/RepositorySourceFuzzer/local"
    })
    @ParameterizedTest
    void installersRejectNestedBuildInputRepositories(
            boolean powershell, String relativeRepository, String relativeInput) throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(true);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        Path repository = source.installed().appHome().resolve(relativeRepository);
        Files.createDirectories(repository);
        run(Map.of(), "git", "init", "-q", repository.toString()).assertSuccess();
        Path input = repository.resolve(relativeInput);
        Files.createDirectories(input.getParent());
        Files.writeString(input, "nested build input");

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output())).contains("source checkout has untracked build inputs");
        assertThat(input).hasContent("nested build input");
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @CsvSource({".ENV, .git", "Config/Application.PROPERTIES, .Git", ".ENV, .GIT"})
    @ParameterizedTest
    @Tag(NATIVE_WINDOWS)
    void powershellSourceUpdateRejectsCaseAliasedConfigurationOnNativeWindows(String relativeInput, String gitDirectory)
            throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(isWindows());
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(true);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(true);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        Path app = source.installed().appHome();
        assertThat(app.resolve(".GIT"))
                .as("the native fixture uses a case-folding filesystem")
                .exists();
        Path intermediateGit = app.resolve("git-case-change");
        Files.move(app.resolve(".git"), intermediateGit);
        Files.move(intermediateGit, app.resolve(gitDirectory));
        run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "config", "core.ignorecase", "false")
                .assertSuccess();
        Path input = app.resolve(relativeInput);
        Files.createDirectories(input.getParent());
        Files.writeString(input, "synthetic.configuration=local");

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output())).contains("source checkout has untracked build inputs");
        assertThat(input).hasContent("synthetic.configuration=local");
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @Tag(NATIVE_WINDOWS)
    @Test
    void powershellSourceUpdateRejectsUnrecordedInputCasingOnNativeWindows() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(isWindows());
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(true);
        SourceDowngrade initial = sourceDowngrade(true);
        initial.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        initial.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        Files.createDirectories(initial.source().resolve("src"));
        addSourceRepositoryCommit(initial.source(), "src/Tracked.java", "record Tracked() {}\n");
        Path app = initial.installed().appHome();
        run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "fetch", initial.source().toString(), "main")
                .assertSuccess();
        run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "checkout", "--detach", "FETCH_HEAD")
                .assertSuccess();
        String head = run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "rev-parse", "HEAD")
                .output()
                .strip();
        var source = new SourceDowngrade(initial.installed(), initial.source(), initial.sentinel(), head);
        run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "config", "core.ignorecase", "true")
                .assertSuccess();
        Path intermediateSource = app.resolve("source-case-change");
        Files.move(app.resolve("src"), intermediateSource);
        Files.move(intermediateSource, app.resolve("SRC"));
        assertThat(run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no")
                        .output())
                .isEmpty();

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output())).contains("unrecorded case-only renames");
        assertThat(app.resolve("SRC/Tracked.java")).hasContent("record Tracked() {}\n");
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @CsvSource({"false, true", "true, true", "false, false", "true, false"})
    @ParameterizedTest
    void installersStopWhenWorkflowMigrationFails(boolean powershell, boolean helpProbe) throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(false);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        source.installed()
                .environment()
                .put(
                        helpProbe ? "SYMPHONY_FAKE_MIGRATION_HELP_FAILURE" : "SYMPHONY_FAKE_WORKFLOW_MIGRATION_FAILURE",
                        "true");

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(source.installed()
                        .binDirectory()
                        .resolve(powershell ? "symphony-trello.ps1.stop" : "symphony-trello.stop"))
                .exists();
        assertThat(Path.of(source.installed().environment().get("SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG")))
                .content(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                .doesNotContain("TrelloBoardSetupMain start", "\"start\"");
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output()))
                .contains(
                        helpProbe
                                ? "simulated migration help failure"
                                : "REJECTED simulated workflow migration rejection")
                .doesNotContain(
                        "cannot check generated workflow bodies",
                        "Symphony for Trello updated.",
                        "Restarting managed workers");
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(booleans = {false, true})
    void installersBuildFromTheCheckoutAndPreserveNonInputFiles(boolean powershell) throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = powershell ? sourceDowngrade(true) : sourceDowngrade(true, "source-downgrade[local]");
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", "1.0.0-rc.1+build.2");
        Path app = source.installed().appHome();
        List<String> retainedPaths = new ArrayList<>(List.of(
                ".mvn/wrapper/dists/local/archive.zip",
                ".mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar",
                ".codex/skills/tessl__local/SKILL.md",
                ".tessl/local",
                "docs/local-note.md",
                ".codex/skills/extra/README.md",
                "config/local-note.txt",
                ".codex/skills/lower/skill.md"));
        if (!Files.exists(app.resolve(".GIT"))) {
            run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "config", "core.ignorecase", "true")
                    .assertSuccess();
            retainedPaths.addAll(List.of(".ENV", "config/Application.PROPERTIES"));
        }
        if (!isWindows()) {
            retainedPaths.add(".codex/skills/tessl:local/SKILL.md");
        }
        for (String path : retainedPaths) {
            Path local = app.resolve(path);
            Files.createDirectories(local.getParent());
            Files.writeString(local, "retained local output");
        }
        if (!isWindows()) {
            Path generated = temporaryDirectory.resolve("generated-skill");
            Files.createDirectories(generated);
            Files.writeString(generated.resolve("SKILL.md"), "generated skill");
            Files.createSymbolicLink(app.resolve(".codex/skills/tessl__linked"), generated);
            Files.createSymbolicLink(app.resolve(".codex/skills/local-note"), generated.resolve("SKILL.md"));
        }
        Files.createDirectories(app.resolve("target"));
        Files.writeString(app.resolve("target/local-output"), "old build output");

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        result.assertSuccess();
        assertThat(Path.of(source.installed().environment().get("SYMPHONY_FAKE_LOG")))
                .content(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                .contains("build-cwd=" + app.toRealPath());
        assertThat(retainedPaths)
                .allSatisfy(path -> assertThat(app.resolve(path)).hasContent("retained local output"));
        assertThat(source.sentinel()).hasContent("original app");
        if (!isWindows()) {
            assertThat(app.resolve(".codex/skills/tessl__linked")).isSymbolicLink();
            assertThat(app.resolve(".codex/skills/local-note")).isSymbolicLink();
        }
    }

    private static List<String> installerLauncher(boolean powershell) {
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        if (powershell) {
            List<String> pwsh = powershellCommand();
            assumeFalse(pwsh.isEmpty());
            return command(pwsh, "-NoProfile", "-File", "./install.ps1");
        }
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        return List.of("bash", "./install.sh");
    }

    @Test
    void posixReleaseInstallRejectsAMajorVersionDowngradeBeforeReplacingTheApp() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        ReleaseArchiveUpdate update = releaseArchiveUpdate("major-downgrade", "99.0.0");

        // when
        ProcessResult result = update.runPosix();

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isEqualTo(SETUP_FAILURE);
        assertThat(result.output())
                .contains(
                        "Symphony 99.0.0 is installed, and release "
                                + installerDefaultRef().substring(1) + " belongs to an older major version.",
                        "Downgrading to an older major version is not supported. Install a 99.x release instead.")
                .doesNotContain("Checking generated workflows...");
        update.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @Test
    void posixReleaseInstallLetsASameMajorDowngradeReachTheReleaseDownload() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("curl"));
        String release = installerDefaultRef().substring(1);
        String newerSameMajor = release.substring(0, release.indexOf('.')) + ".99.0";
        ReleaseArchiveUpdate update = releaseArchiveUpdate("same-major-downgrade", newerSameMajor);

        // when
        ProcessResult result = update.runPosix();

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode())
                .as("the release directory is empty, so the download must fail after the version check passed")
                .isNotZero();
        assertThat(result.output())
                .contains(
                        "[2/2] Updating Symphony", update.releaseBaseUrl() + "/symphony-trello-" + release + ".tar.gz")
                .doesNotContain("belongs to an older major version");
        update.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(booleans = {false, true})
    void posixSourceInstallRejectsAMajorDowngradeWithoutTheOldWrapper(boolean newCommandDirectory) throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        SourceDowngrade update = sourceDowngrade(false);
        ReleaseArchiveUpdate installed = update.installed();
        if (newCommandDirectory) {
            installed = new ReleaseArchiveUpdate(
                    installed.appHome(),
                    installed.binDirectory().resolveSibling("replacement-bin"),
                    installed.environment());
            update = new SourceDowngrade(installed, update.source(), update.sentinel(), update.originalHead());
        } else {
            Files.delete(installed.binDirectory().resolve("symphony-trello"));
        }

        // when
        ProcessResult result = update.run(List.of("bash", "./install.sh"));

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isEqualTo(SETUP_FAILURE);
        assertThat(result.output()).contains("belongs to an older major version");
        update.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(booleans = {false, true})
    void posixSourceInstallRejectsAMajorDowngradeHiddenByDirtyPom(boolean staged) throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        SourceDowngrade update = dirtySourceDowngrade(staged);

        // when
        ProcessResult result = update.run(List.of("bash", "./install.sh"));

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isEqualTo(SETUP_FAILURE);
        assertThat(result.output()).contains("source checkout has local changes");
        update.assertAppUntouched();
        assertThat(update.installed().appHome().resolve("pom.xml"))
                .hasContent("<project><version>1.0.0</version></project>\n");
    }

    @CsvSource({
        "false, assume-unchanged, pom.xml, true", "true, assume-unchanged, pom.xml, true",
        "false, assume-unchanged, pom.xml, false", "true, assume-unchanged, pom.xml, false",
        "false, skip-worktree, pom.xml, true", "true, skip-worktree, pom.xml, true",
        "false, skip-worktree, pom.xml, false", "true, skip-worktree, pom.xml, false",
        "false, both, pom.xml, true", "true, both, pom.xml, true",
        "false, both, pom.xml, false", "true, both, pom.xml, false",
        "false, assume-unchanged, WORKFLOW.example.md, true", "true, assume-unchanged, WORKFLOW.example.md, true",
        "false, skip-worktree, WORKFLOW.example.md, true", "true, skip-worktree, WORKFLOW.example.md, true"
    })
    @ParameterizedTest
    void installersRejectTrackedChangesHiddenByIndexFlags(
            boolean powershell, String flag, String relativePath, boolean modified) throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(true);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        addSourceRepositoryCommit(source.source(), "pom.xml", "<project><version>1.0.0</version></project>\n");
        Path app = source.installed().appHome();
        String firstFlag = flag.equals("both") ? "assume-unchanged" : flag;
        run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "update-index", "--" + firstFlag, "--", relativePath)
                .assertSuccess();
        if (flag.equals("both")) {
            run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "update-index", "--skip-worktree", "--", relativePath)
                    .assertSuccess();
        }
        Path localFile = app.resolve(relativePath);
        String localContent = modified ? "<project><version>88.0.0</version></project>\n" : Files.readString(localFile);
        Files.writeString(localFile, localContent);
        ProcessResult status =
                run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=no");
        status.assertSuccess();
        assertThat(status.output()).isEmpty();

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output()))
                .contains("tracked files use assume-unchanged or skip-worktree", "Clear those index flags");
        assertThat(localFile).hasContent(localContent);
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(booleans = {false, true})
    void installersStopWhenIndexFlagsCannotBeInspected(boolean powershell) throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeFalse(isWindows());
        List<String> launcher = installerLauncher(powershell);
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(true);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        source.installed().environment().put("SYMPHONY_FAKE_VERSION", FAKE_APP_VERSION);
        ProcessResult gitLocation = run(Map.of(), "bash", "-c", "command -v git");
        gitLocation.assertSuccess();
        Path fakeBin = temporaryDirectory.resolve("index-inspection-bin");
        Files.createDirectories(fakeBin);
        writeExecutable(
                fakeBin.resolve("git"),
                """
                #!/usr/bin/env bash
                if [[ "${3:-}" == ls-files && "${4:-}" == -v ]]; then exit 37; fi
                exec %s "$@"
                """
                        .formatted(shellQuote(gitLocation.output().strip())));
        source.installed()
                .environment()
                .put(
                        "PATH",
                        fakeBin
                                + File.pathSeparator
                                + source.installed().environment().get("PATH"));

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(launcher);

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isNotZero();
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output())).contains("cannot inspect source checkout index flags");
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    private SourceDowngrade dirtySourceDowngrade(boolean staged) throws Exception {
        SourceDowngrade update = sourceDowngrade(true);
        addSourceRepositoryCommit(update.source(), "pom.xml", "<project><version>99.0.0</version></project>\n");
        Path app = update.installed().appHome();
        run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "fetch", update.source().toString(), "main")
                .assertSuccess();
        run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "checkout", "--detach", "FETCH_HEAD")
                .assertSuccess();
        String head = run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "rev-parse", "HEAD")
                .output()
                .strip();
        Files.writeString(app.resolve("pom.xml"), "<project><version>1.0.0</version></project>\n");
        if (staged) {
            run(Map.of(), "git", "-C", app.toString(), "add", "pom.xml").assertSuccess();
        }
        return new SourceDowngrade(update.installed(), update.source(), update.sentinel(), head);
    }

    @MethodSource("sourcePomVersions")
    @ParameterizedTest
    void posixSourcePreflightReadsOnlyAnExplicitRootVersion(SourcePom sourcePom) throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));

        // then

        assertThat(result.output())
                .contains(sourcePom.expectedMessage(), "Installer stopped during [2/2] Updating Symphony.");
        update.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @CsvSource({"437, valid", "1252, valid", "65001, valid", "437, invalid", "1252, git-failure", "1252, mojibake"})
    @ParameterizedTest
    void powershellSourcePreflightReadsUtf8PomWithScopedConsoleEncodingWhenAvailable(int codePage, String scenario)
            throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> pwsh = powershellCommand();
        assumeFalse(pwsh.isEmpty());
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        SourceDowngrade source = sourceDowngrade(false);
        source.recordWorkerStopRequests();
        String pom =
                switch (scenario) {
                    case "invalid" -> "<project />";
                    case "mojibake" -> "\u00EF\u00BB\u00BF<project><version>1.5.0</version></project>";
                    default ->
                        "\uFEFF<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"UTF-8\"?><project><version>1.5.0</version></project>";
                };
        addSourceRepositoryCommit(source.source(), "pom.xml", pom);
        Path encodingRecord = temporaryDirectory.resolve("console-encoding.txt");
        Path launcher = temporaryDirectory.resolve("encoded-console-install.ps1");
        String gitFailure = scenario.equals("git-failure")
                ? """
                  $nativeGit = @(Get-Command git -CommandType Application)[0].Source
                  function git {
                    if ($args.Count -ge 3 -and $args[2] -eq 'show') {
                      & $nativeGit -C $args[1] show 'synthetic-missing-ref:pom.xml'
                      return
                    }
                    & $nativeGit @args
                  }
                  """
                : "";
        Files.writeString(
                launcher,
                """
                [Console]::OutputEncoding = [Text.Encoding]::GetEncoding(%s)
                %s
                & %s @args
                $installerExit = $LASTEXITCODE
                [IO.File]::WriteAllText(%s, [Console]::OutputEncoding.CodePage.ToString())
                exit $installerExit
                """
                        .formatted(
                                codePage,
                                gitFailure,
                                powerShellLiteral(
                                        Path.of("install.ps1").toAbsolutePath().toString()),
                                powerShellLiteral(encodingRecord.toString())));

        // when
        ProcessResult result = source.run(command(pwsh, "-NoProfile", "-File", launcher.toString()));

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isOne();
        String expectedMessage =
                switch (scenario) {
                    case "invalid" -> "cannot read source project/version";
                    case "git-failure" -> "cannot read source pom.xml";
                    case "mojibake" -> "Data at the root level is invalid";
                    default -> "release 1.5.0 belongs to an older major version";
                };
        assertThat(normalizedWhitespace(result.output())).contains(expectedMessage);
        assertThat(encodingRecord).hasContent(Integer.toString(codePage));
        source.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @Test
    void posixSourcePreflightKeepsTheFailureWhenTemporaryCheckoutCleanupFails() throws Exception {
        // given
        assumeTrue(commandExists("bash"));
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        SourceDowngrade update = sourceDowngrade(false);
        addSourceRepositoryCommit(update.source(), "pom.xml", "<project />\n");
        Path fakeBin = temporaryDirectory.resolve("cleanup-failure-bin");
        Files.createDirectories(fakeBin);
        writeExecutable(fakeBin.resolve("rm"), "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexit 17\n");
        update.installed()
                .environment()
                .put(
                        "PATH",
                        fakeBin
                                + File.pathSeparator
                                + update.installed().environment().get("PATH"));
        update.installed().environment().put("TMPDIR", temporaryDirectory.toString());

        // when
        ProcessResult result = update.run(List.of("bash", "./install.sh"));

        // then
        assertThat(result.exitCode()).as(result.output()).isEqualTo(SETUP_FAILURE);
        assertThat(result.output())
                .contains(
                        "cannot read source project/version",
                        "Installer stopped during [2/2] Updating Symphony.",
                        "cannot remove temporary source checkout",
                        "remove it manually after the installer stops");
        update.assertAppUntouched();
    }

    @Test
    void powershellSourcePreflightKeepsTheFailureWhenTemporaryCheckoutCleanupFailsWhenAvailable() throws Exception {
        // given
        List<String> pwsh = powershellCommand();
        assumeFalse(pwsh.isEmpty());
        assumeTrue(commandExists("git"));
        SourceDowngrade update = sourceDowngrade(false);

                installer,
